MMO Idle Theoretical DPS Report

External balance/debug report generated from shared item, skill, monster, stat, DoT, empowered, upgrade, and weapon-family formulas. This is not an in-game panel and not a combat simulator. It omits movement, enemies attacking back, deaths, sustain, AoE, pathing, aggro, and eHP.

Unlock model: T1 class root, T2 frame, T3 close/far range, T4 path/spec. Weapons: each report tier's non-tutorial weapons at +3. Targets use biome spawn pools one tier below the report tier, excluding tutorial/test monsters. Mechanics with runtime state are represented as deterministic steady-state estimates and are called out in row notes. Conduit and its subclasses are excluded.

25 samples. Uses weapon tier 1; class unlock tier 0. Target dummy from 10 non-boss monsters in biome tier 1 fallback: HP 143, plating 0.80, DR 1.50%.
